{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"slicker","tags":["comparative"]},{"form":"slickest","tags":["superlative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"This rain is making the roads slick.","type":"example"},{"text":"The top coating of lacquer gives this finish a slick look.","type":"example"},{"text":"His large round head was shaved slick.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Slippery or smooth due to a covering of liquid; often used to describe appearances."]},{"glosses":["Sleek; smooth."]},{"examples":[{"text":"They read all kinds of slick magazines.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Appearing expensive or sophisticated."]},{"examples":[{"text":"That new sales rep is slick.
